## `ALB-1610-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `ALB` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Moisture-Warp Inspector
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Sawn Timber Dimensional Tolerance, Moisture & Warp Defect
- **Intro:** Moisture-Warp Inspector — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Sawn Timber Dimensional Tolerance, Moisture & Warp Defect — Verify Steady-State Line Conditions: Conveyor speed: 12 m/min ± 0.5 m/min; Kiln temperature: 85°C ± 2°C; Ambient RH: 45% ± 5%; Target MC: 12% ± 1%.
- **Key tolerance:** Conveyor speed: 12 m/min ± 0.5 m/min; Kiln temperature: 85°C ± 2°C; Ambient RH: 45% ± 5%; Target MC: 12% ± 1%.
- **Standards:** `ISO 4470:1981`, `ISO 3130:1975`, `ISO 4859:1982`, `DIN 68121-1:1993`
